  Is WQ at g7 defended?

  e6 captures at d7, f7.
  h4 captures at g5.
  No white pawn defends g7.
  So if BK moves to h7 and WQ moves to g7, BK can just capture the queen!
  BK at h7, WQ at g7. BK can take WQ (h7xg7).
  Then it's not mate!
